Use when comparing multiple models/datasets/categories in one sweep, or authoring/editing a benchmark config YAML. For turning measured results into README/docs benchmark tables, see the benchmark-and-docs-refresh skill. Do not use for regular single-model training (see anomalib-training) or the multi-model benchmarking pipeline (see anomalib-benchmarking). - **anr-watchdog-triage**: Diagnose hangs and OS kills that never throw: Android ANRs, iOS watchdog terminations and hangs, and memory-pressure kills such as jetsam. Map each kill to its cause first — an ANR trace whose main thread is blocked on a lock, disk I/O or a binder call; a watchdog kill because launch or resume ran out of time; a memory kill driven by peak footprint, not necessarily a leak — then read the main-thread stack, move blocking work off the main thread, and judge memory from a footprint timeline, not one heap snapshot.
